THIRD-PARTY NOTICES

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. Document fonts embedded in the layout engine
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

These faces are compiled into `casual-doc-layout` with `include_bytes!`, so a
binary built from this repository carries their bytes. The one exception is
Roboto, whose four blobs the `external-web-fonts` WebAssembly build omits in
favour of faces the browser fetches; the files themselves are committed either
way. `crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/README.md` records each file's provenance
and the role the font resolver gives it.

Roboto
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/Roboto-{Regular,Bold,Italic,BoldItalic}.ttf
  Copyright 2015 Google Inc. All Rights Reserved.
  Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 -- the licence record carried in
  the font's own `name` table (IDs 13 and 14). Its text is the LICENSE file at the
  root of this repository.

Caladea
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/Caladea-{Regular,Bold,Italic,BoldItalic}.ttf
  Copyright (c) 2012 Huerta Tipografia
  Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 -- the licence record carried in
  the font's own `name` table (IDs 13 and 14). Its text is the LICENSE file at the
  root of this repository.

Carlito
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/Carlito-{Regular,Bold,Italic,BoldItalic}.ttf
  Copyright 2013 The Carlito Project Authors
  (https://github.com/googlefonts/carlito), with Reserved Font Name "Carlito"
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/LICENSES/OFL-1.1-Carlito.txt

Liberation Sans, Liberation Serif, Liberation Mono
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/liberation/Liberation{Sans,Serif,Mono}-*.ttf
  Digitized data copyright (c) 2010 Google Corporation, with Reserved Font Arimo,
  Tinos and Cousine.
  Copyright (c) 2012 Red Hat, Inc., with Reserved Font Name Liberation.
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/LICENSES/OFL-1.1-Liberation.txt

Noto Emoji
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/NotoEmoji-Variable.ttf
  Copyright 2013 Google LLC
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/LICENSES/OFL-1.1-NotoEmoji.txt

Noto Sans Symbols 2 (subset)
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/NotoSansSymbols2-Subset.ttf
  Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/symbols)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  crates/casual-doc-layout/fonts/LICENSES/OFL-1.1-NotoSansSymbols2.txt

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. Interface fonts served with the web application
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

These faces are self-hosted -- `webapp/src/fonts.css` references them from this
repository and never from a font CDN, which `webapp/tests/chrome_fonts.test.mjs`
enforces. They are the chrome's own typefaces and are not used to lay out a
document.

Inter
  webapp/assets/fonts/inter-latin-400-700.woff2
  Copyright (c) 2016 The Inter Project Authors (https://github.com/rsms/inter)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/LICENSE-Inter.txt

Instrument Sans
  webapp/assets/fonts/instrument-sans.woff2, instrument-sans-italic.woff2
  Copyright 2022 The Instrument Sans Project Authors
  (https://github.com/Instrument/instrument-sans)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/LICENSE-Instrument-Sans.txt

Newsreader
  webapp/assets/fonts/newsreader.woff2, newsreader-italic.woff2
  Copyright 2020 The Newsreader Project Authors
  (http://github.com/productiontype/Newsreader)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/LICENSE-Newsreader.txt

JetBrains Mono
  webapp/assets/fonts/jetbrains-mono.woff2
  Copyright 2020 The JetBrains Mono Project Authors
  (https://github.com/JetBrains/JetBrainsMono)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/LICENSE-JetBrains-Mono.txt

Material Symbols Outlined
  webapp/assets/fonts/material-symbols-outlined.woff2
  Copyright 2026 Google LLC. All Rights Reserved.
  Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
  webapp/assets/fonts/LICENSE-Material-Symbols.txt

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. Spelling word lists
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

SCOWL (Spell Checker Oriented Word Lists)
  webapp/dict/en-US.txt, webapp/dict/en-GB.txt -- generated from the
  `scowl-2020.12.07` release by `webapp/tools/build-dictionary.mjs`; the release,
  its URL and its SHA-256 are recorded in webapp/dict/README.md.
  The collective work is Copyright 2000-2018 by Kevin Atkinson, together with the
  further copyright holders named in the licence file itself.
  webapp/dict/LICENSE.SCOWL.txt

PP-OCRv5 mobile text detection and Latin recognition models (PaddleOCR)
  docs/assets/ocr/det.onnx, docs/assets/ocr/latin_rec.onnx,
  docs/assets/ocr/latin_rec.dict.txt -- converted from Paddle inference format
  to ONNX, weights unchanged; provenance in docs/assets/ocr/NOTICE.txt.
  Copyright PaddlePaddle Authors (https://github.com/PaddlePaddle/PaddleOCR).
  Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
  docs/assets/ocr/LICENSE-Apache-2.0.txt

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. Document fonts self-hosted and served from this origin
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

These faces lay out DOCUMENT text, not the chrome. They are not embedded in the
engine: `webapp/src/web_fonts.mjs` fetches them from this repository's own origin
at runtime and registers them through the font-registry seam. They are committed
here, so this repository redistributes them. Each is redistributed unmodified,
and the licence claim is verified from the file's own `name` table (IDs 13/14).
Provenance, byte counts and SHA-256 digests are in
`webapp/assets/fonts/script/README.md`.

Roboto (variable, OFL build)
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/Roboto-Variable.ttf, Roboto-Italic-Variable.ttf
  Copyright 2011 The Roboto Project Authors
  (https://github.com/googlefonts/roboto-classic)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/OFL-1.1-Roboto.txt
  NOTE: this is the OFL-licensed Roboto that Google Fonts now publishes. The
  Roboto embedded in the layout engine (section 1) is the older Apache-2.0
  build and a different file; the two licences must not be conflated.

Noto Sans
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/NotoSans-Variable.ttf, NotoSans-Italic-Variable.ttf
  Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors
  (https://github.com/notofonts/latin-greek-cyrillic)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/OFL-1.1-NotoSans.txt

Noto Serif
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/NotoSerif-Variable.ttf, NotoSerif-Italic-Variable.ttf
  Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors
  (https://github.com/notofonts/latin-greek-cyrillic)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/OFL-1.1-NotoSerif.txt

Noto Sans script families -- Arabic, Devanagari, Bengali, Gurmukhi, Gujarati,
Oriya, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, Malayalam, Sinhala, Hebrew, Thai
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/NotoSans{Arabic,Devanagari,Bengali,Gurmukhi,
  Gujarati,Oriya,Tamil,Telugu,Kannada,Malayalam,Sinhala,Hebrew,
  Thai}-Regular.ttf
  Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/)
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  webapp/assets/fonts/script/OFL-1.1-Noto.txt

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
5. Test-only font fixtures
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Font data that exists only so a test can create a condition. Not served to any
reader and not embedded in the engine. See `fixtures/fonts/README.md`, which
records the exact subsetting command, so the derivation is reproducible.

Noto Sans CJK SC (19-codepoint test subset)
  fixtures/fonts/NotoSansCJK-TestSubset.otf
  (c) 2014-2021 Adobe (http://www.adobe.com/) -- the copyright record carried in
  the font's own `name` table (ID 0). Published by The Noto Project
  (https://github.com/notofonts/noto-cjk); Noto is a trademark of Google Inc.
  SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1
  fixtures/fonts/OFL-1.1-NotoSansCJK.txt
  MODIFIED: subset to 19 code points with `fontTools.subset`, which the OFL
  permits. This face declares NO Reserved Font Name -- the upstream OFL text
  states none -- so the RFN restriction on renaming a modified font does not
  apply. The file is still named `NotoSansCJK-TestSubset` so that nothing in
  the tree can mistake a 19-glyph test fixture for the 16.4 MB upstream face.
  It is never served to a reader.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6. Fonts fetched at runtime, which this repository does not redistribute
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Four faces are too large to commit -- the three Noto Sans CJK region faces
(~16.4 MB each) and the COLRv1 Noto Color Emoji face (4.99 MB), 51.8 MB
together. `webapp/src/web_fonts.mjs` serves them from this origin when a
deployment has provisioned them with `webapp/tools/provision-script-fonts.mjs`,
and otherwise falls back to a commit-pinned upstream mirror. Either way the
bytes are verified against a SHA-256 in the manifest before they are used.

Because they are not committed, they carry no attribution above: they are
distributed by their own publishers, under the SIL Open Font License 1.1, and
each arrives with the licence records in its own `name` table. A deployment that
provisions them IS redistributing them and should ship the OFL text alongside;
`webapp/assets/fonts/script/OFL-1.1-Noto.txt` is that text. A host that would
rather supply its own faces does so through the same font-registry seam.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7. Standard data embedded in the engine
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ECMA-376 DrawingML preset shape definitions
  crates/casual-doc-model/data/preset_shapes.txt -- generated by
  `crates/casual-doc-import/examples/generate_preset_shapes.rs` from the
  ECMA-376 Part 1 annex `presetShapeDefinitions.xml`, as redistributed in the
  Apache POI project's Apache-2.0 release; the source copy, its URL and its
  SHA-256 are recorded in crates/casual-doc-model/data/README.md. Compiled into
  `casual-doc-model` with `include_str!`, so a binary built from this
  repository carries it.
  (c) Ecma International. Used under Ecma's copyright licence, clause (iv)
  (implementation in a standard-conformant product), whose full text -- as the
  licence requires -- accompanies the file:
  crates/casual-doc-model/data/LICENSE-ECMA-376.txt


================================================================

OpenDoc MODIFICATIONS.txt
OpenDoc source snapshot 5ac261ceadbb0fa0bcbe729ce3c2e20721756949.
Build manifests were narrowed by Lonskiy Yegor to the PDF importer and DOCX writer. Development dependencies were removed.
In casual-doc-pdf-import/src/reflow/mod.rs, Lonskiy Yegor narrowed the repeated-text and repeated-object header/footer bands to preserve body headings and tables near the top of the page.
The application supplies its own browser worker and source-layout repair.
In casual-doc-pdf-import/src/reflow/tables.rs, Lonskiy Yegor added short-sentence detection to reduce false table detection in parallel prose columns.
In casual-doc-pdf-import/src/reflow/emit.rs, Lonskiy Yegor records source page changes within running paragraphs and writes them as editable DOCX page breaks.
Lonskiy Yegor normalized compatible column regions and moved editable column breaks, source page starts and borderless table geometry into the document model.
The importer includes source coordinates and stable line heights for body paragraphs and keeps text separated by independent prose in the editable flow. The emitter preserves measured spacing between adjacent paragraphs with stable line heights in the same column.
The emitter places body paragraph and first-line indentation in the actual exported column, carries measured vertical section margins, and omits blank paragraphs from source geometry evidence.
Running page fields keep the source number's digit width, and a printed page count becomes an editable field only when it matches the source document's length.
The PDF importer excludes glyphs and annotation metadata from clipped or covered regions that the browser preserves as artwork. The remaining page content reflows as editable text.
The PDF reader applies the page's UserUnit to displayed dimensions, glyphs, graphics, annotation geometry and revision transforms.
The browser import uses measured gaps between painted lines instead of converting whitespace-only paint into Word paragraphs.
List paragraphs use the source marker font, label position and continuation indent relative to the exported Word section.
Borderless tables retain wrapped text painted between aligned rows inside its source column.
Paragraphs at different type sizes use their own exact line boxes to preserve measured baseline spacing.
Rotated labels retain their source orientation through grouping so anchored marginal text does not consume a body's spacing before tables.
Borderless table grids preserve the outer horizontal offset and keep every internal column edge within its measured gutter.
The Word writer explicitly records the line style of underlined text, including single underlines on links.
The reflow importer keeps separate linked lines and preserves literal hyphens in printed web address paths.
Ruled table cells preserve paragraph boundaries between a printed label and a value on the following line when the source typography or spacing separates them.
Accessible tab markers on printed PDF glyphs become spaces in editable text runs. Geometric tab stops remain explicit Word tab elements.
The importer recognises a parenthesised total after a running page number and writes it as a Word page-count field.
